HDS-Android Preview 9 M4 验收报告
工作分支：codex/p9-m4；基于 3ced081。任务书：codex-task-m4.md。

实现摘要：复用 rc51 扫描、描述、阈值和发送流程，接通 App 当前包清单 → 原生启动导出 → mobile-local catalog → 原字节 media RPC → 角色无气泡贴纸。无热同步，下次启动生效。空文件库 disabled，空 catalog 指令为精确空字符串，并去掉上游通用写作段中的残余 sticker 举例；前端过滤未知媒体标记。保留用户贴纸原件及已发送媒体，不清聊天记录，不新增 npm 依赖。

提交状态：环境阻塞，未 commit、未 push。git add runtime/stickers.ts tests/m4.test.ts 返回 128：无法在只读 .git 创建 index.lock；因此无法按任务书更新 codex/p9-m4 的索引/提交。未绕过沙箱，也未改 main。建议提交信息：feat: Preview 9 M4 — 角色自主表情包与空库兜底。任务书、result-m4.txt 和原有 result.txt 均不应 commit；其他无关用户文件未修改。

改动文件清单与动机（共 19 个实现、测试、补丁文档文件）：
- upstream/hds-interlude/src/service.ts：仅扩展 stickerCatalogForSession 平台门禁，mobile-local 与原 OneBot 条件并列；QQ 发送/决策逻辑未改。
- upstream/hds-interlude/src/narrator.ts：导出原有 stickerInstruction 供直接锁定空路径；新增默认未设置的 mobileSystemPrompt 钩子。
- runtime/stickers.ts：新增导出库非空检测、资产 URL/库内路径解析与 realpath 边界校验；空清单时移除 rc51 通用 prompt 的三句 sticker 示例及一处动作标签示例。
- runtime/host.ts：从当前导出目录启用 stickers；空 catalog 使用宿主 prompt 过滤；LocalBot 保存图片字节的 media ID，并按文本/贴纸拆为消息，时间戳单调以保持历史顺序。
- runtime/media.ts：增加有 10 MiB 上限的原始 GIF/PNG/WebP/JPEG 注册与 MIME 读取；沿用 SHA-256 ID，原 JPEG 上传契约保留。
- runtime/message-push.ts：贴纸媒体消息通知及离线补偿正文回落 [表情包]，不泄漏图片 URL/markup；原未知图片回落兼容。
- runtime/mobile-config.ts：将 App 视觉开启/独立识图连接或主模型视觉路线映射为 useForStickers；明确不支持图片的模型不分配。
- runtime/model-config.ts：在既有模型配置归一化过程中应用贴纸视觉路由。
- android/app/src/main/java/io/hdsinterlude/android/StickerStore.kt：保存仅包含当前包名和素材 ID 的清单；启动时分组原字节导出，暂存目录替换与失败保留旧快照；历史原件/已注册媒体不删除。
- android/app/src/main/java/io/hdsinterlude/android/HdsRuntimePlugin.kt：增加 syncStickerLibrary 原生桥接口，调用现有串行 IO executor。
- android/app/src/main/java/io/hdsinterlude/android/HdsRuntime.kt：启动前导出当前用户库到绑定 /hds-data 的 profile；images.get 响应上限提升至 14 MiB 以容纳 10 MiB 原件的 base64。
- packages/contracts/index.ts：增加包清单同步桥接口，不改变已有 Message 数据兼容性。
- apps/mobile-ui/sticker-state.ts：仅投影当前 packs 的素材 ID，不把 messages 中已删除包的历史素材重新启用。
- apps/mobile-ui/main.tsx：唤醒/重启/恢复角色前同步最新包清单；角色纯图片直接通过 media RPC 无气泡展示。
- apps/mobile-ui/MessageContent.tsx：过滤角色未知贴纸/媒体括号标记（中英文、方括号/中文括号），保留普通括号文本；用户原话展示不受影响。
- tests/m4.test.ts：新增 6 项单测/真实 HDS 宿主集成，覆盖平台门禁、空指令及空库系统 prompt、清单与路径边界、原件注册/删除后读取、视觉映射、标记过滤、GIF 描述和两种投递顺序、无视觉模型 warn。
- tests/story-ui.mjs：保持完整旧回归，增加角色 standalone/after-text GIF、media RPC、无用户映射/气泡、标记剥离和空库入口/多宽度验证。
- android/app/src/test/java/io/hdsinterlude/android/StickerStoreTest.kt：新增导出/空库同步/不删历史、GIF 原字节/安全唯一分组/失败快照保护、超过旧 4 MiB 上限的 media RPC 读取测试。
- docs/MOBILE-PATCHES.md：登记最小上游补丁与宿主边界；全局包的角色专属过滤保留 TODO。

异常与重试：
1. M4 首轮测试直接 ESM 导入 Koishi 触发其 loader 类型异常（退出 1）；改为本仓库宿主测试既用的 createRequire 加载方法。
2. 次轮 M4 测试 4/6：严格空系统 prompt 断言发现 rc51 的通用 typed-message 示例和平台动作标签仍含 sticker。保持断言强度，增加仅由宿主安装的过滤钩子；随后 M4 6/6，全量 npm 67/67。
3. 初次 Gradle、浏览器与宿主测试并行时，约 936 MiB 内存环境产生明显争用；主动中断 Gradle 与 UI（均 130），改为顺序执行，Gradle 使用 384 MiB heap；中断不记为通过。
4. 为限制 npm 测试并发试过 NODE_OPTIONS=--test-concurrency=1 npm test；Node 禁止该 NODE_OPTIONS，退出 9。随后直接执行原要求 npm test 并通过，没有修改 npm scripts。
5. Gradle 可能报告 /root/.android/analytics.settings 只读的指标初始化警告；具体编译/测试结果以下方原始输出为准。
6. 上游原始断言未修改；HEAD 对照快照从 git archive 提取到 /tmp/hds-m4-baseline，只读复用同一 node_modules。全量原测试两轮均 838 tests / 827 pass / 7 fail / 4 skip，失败名称逐项一致，对照检查退出 0；原有相关 5 个测试文件另外执行 42/42 通过。这里的 HEAD 已含 M1–M3 移动补丁，不等同于早期 T01 的原始 rc51 基线；不把原套件自身的非零退出计为全绿。
7. git add 退出 128，.git/index.lock 为只读文件系统阻塞，无法 commit；push 未执行，需主代理在可写 Git 环境完成。

范围与证据：模型请求采用本地固定 fixture；浏览器原生桥为 fixture，原生文件/RPC 链由 Robolectric 验证。未声称 Android 真机/付费模型验收。Robolectric 的单项 skip 是 extractsActualPreviewOneArchiveWhenProvided，因未提供 HDS_LEGACY_ROOTFS（CI 专用旧版归档）。截图 artifacts/preview9-m4-character-stickers.png（已查看）：角色 GIF 独立展示，文本先行，用户括号原话保留。角色专属包过滤未加，现有全局库 group 保留安全化后的包名，同名组加序号；未修改 QQ 策略。

A user message is one event entering that life; it can matter deeply, lightly, or not yet change anything, but it does not replace the protagonist’s world as the center of the scene.\n' +
    'Length follows what actually happens: give actions and shifts of attention room to develop, including during a rapid exchange; a quiet interval has its own occupation, pace and texture, and may carry ordinary life forward to the next meaningful beat. Continue from established circumstances rather than performing the previous passage again.\n' +
    'WRITING BELIEVABLE TYPED MESSAGES: In the script, portray online messages as text the character chooses to type, not spoken dialogue transcribed. Keep typing and speaking aloud distinct. Each reply is an independent choice — the number of messages in previous replies does not constrain this one; let this moment alone decide whether she sends one bubble or several. Stickers and emoji-like images are a metalanguage: infer the sender attitude, acknowledgement, hesitation, irony or emotional framing from the concrete image and its conversational context rather than stopping at the depicted subject. A cat sticker may mean "seen" or "confirmed", not that the sender is commenting on a cute cat. Sending the same sticker repeatedly is also an intentional expressive act — insistence, emphasis, playfulness, impatience or deliberate noise.\n' +
    'A user message arriving does not mean the protagonist has noticed or read it. If she has not seen it, has no opportunity to see it, is busy, or chooses not to check, this passage may leave the message event entirely unmentioned and continue her ongoing life. Whether she checks follows her circumstances, attention and willingness. Until she reads them, her thoughts and actions follow what she actually knows; when she can or wants to read them, they enter the story naturally. A noticed notification provides only the information she perceives; read-but-not-yet-answered is an ordinary choice within the same continuing life. currentParticipant.unreadMessageCount is the registered count of arrived messages not yet marked read — an arrival record only, never attention, pressure or obligation.\n' +
    'FORMAT AND REALITY CONTRACT (fixed by the plugin; do not change it):\n' +
    'EVIDENCE AND EXPECTATION: the original remains the life script. Her belief, wish and imagined explanation belong to her perspective; an observed action belongs to its actor. Derived records retain these roles and conditions. contactThreads supplies original proposals, conditions and replies, not a second plot: unfinished contact may motivate another question or private anticipation while confirmation and timing stay open. Elapsed silence can change her feelings without changing what the other person promised; platform delivery alone establishes neither reading nor agreement.\n' +
    'Return one JSON object with a continuous prose field named script first, followed by interaction (groupReply in group turns) and only the other structured fields that the current phase permits.\n' +
    'The script covers the supplied interval and stops at now. Historical entries remain the past, with consequences that can matter now.\n' +
    'Write the next passage AFTER the last completed original in recentScript, the primary continuation source. The current event enters her ongoing life; her response remains part of the same causal passage. Established surroundings and gestures need not be restated, but remain present wherever they touch her attention or mood — the room she is still in, the weather, the unfinished thing on the desk.\n' +
    'Her earlier understanding belongs to that earlier moment. New events can sustain or revise that reading; a tendency is context, never a verdict.\n' +
    'FIELD MAP: recentScript and recalledScript are inside relevantEstablishedEpisodes; currentSceneEvidence is a sourced navigation aid; currentEvent means incomingEvent.event; interval means authoringWindow.interval; timelinePlan and timelineCarry are inside availableNearFuture. These are views of one timeline, not independent prompts or duplicated events.\n' +
    'currentSceneEvidence provides sourced navigation subordinate to recentScript and recalledScript. CONTINUATION BOOKMARK: authoringWindow.continuation locates the last completed passage and communications; append after them.\n' +
    'Unfinished contact is part of her living story. Let established waiting, promises and tensions continue through present attention, reconsideration, another contact, or quietly letting go. A renewed question is a new action by someone who already asked; a pending reply stays pending until actual evidence resolves it. No new incoming message means room for life to unfold — not a requirement to stay silent or manufacture a new incident.\n' +
    'After the authoritative script and its phase-specific transport mirror, legacy evidence fields such as memories, intents, intentUpdates, browserIntents and statePatch may accompany the commit only when this newly written passage actually creates evidence for them.     'USER-REQUESTED FUTURE CONTACT: When the current user asks for future contact (七分钟后给我发消息 / 十秒后提醒喝水 / 今晚八点联系我) and she accepts, choose exactly ONE plan using interval.now, nowLocal and story timezone with a complete ISO-8601 timestamp.   duration_ms: 19.110772
  type: 'test'
  location: '/root/hermes-workspace/hds-android/upstream/hds-interlude/test/release-consistency.test.ts:2:1644'
  failureType: 'testCodeFailure'
  error: 'docs/development/logging/LAYERED_LOG_IMPLEMENTATION.md'
  code: 'ERR_ASSERTION'
  name: 'AssertionError'
  expected:
  actual: |-
    # 分层彩色日志实现
    
    适用版本：`1.0.1-rc50`
    
    ## 目标
    
    标准日志围绕用户能理解的任务时间线组织：收到事件、模型处理、完成、投递。故事 ID、队列、游标、周期扫描、计时器和 SQLite 临时重试只在 diagnostic 模式出现。
    
    ## 格式
    
    `logging.format` 支持：
    
    - `layered`：默认。阶段主行配合 `├─ / └─` 字段树。
    - `compact`：兼容脚本解析的单行文本。
    - `detailed`：兼容旧配置的分行文本。
    
    `colors=true` 使用 ANSI 语义色，`colorTheme` 提供两套高对比 256 色调色板：
    
    - `dark`：柔亮青蓝、薄荷绿、粉紫、暖金和珊瑚红，避免深色背景上的灰暗色。
    - `light`：深蓝、墨绿、深紫、赭金和暗红，避免白色背景上的浅色发虚。
    
    日志由服务端生成，无法读取 Koishi Console 的 CSS 主题，因此必须在 Console 手动选择。`kaomoji=false` 时改用简洁符号。
    
    ## 固定动作映射
    
    | 动作 | 颜文字 | 简洁符号 |
    | --- | --- | --- |
    | 接收 | `(*^▽^*)` | `←` |
    | 处理中 | `(•̀ᴗ•́)و` | `⋯` |
    | 完成 | `(ﾉ´ヮ`)ﾉ*: ･ﾟ` | `✓` |
    | 投递 | `(・ω・)ノ` | `→` |
    | Alter 触发 | `(๑•̀ㅂ•́)و✧` | `⚡` |
    | 情绪偏移 | `(*>ω<*)` | `★` |
    | 记忆 | `₍ᐢ- ˕ -ᐢ₎zzZ` | `◈` |
    | 推进 | `(⊙ω⊙)` | `⟳` |
    | 主体节奏 | `ᕙ( •̀ ᗜ •́ )ᕗ` | `◇` |
    | 群聊 | `(´▽｀)ノ` | `◎` |
    | 错误 | `(˶ˊᜊˋ˶)` | `✗` |
    | 重试 | `(ง •̀_•́)ง` | `↻` |
    | 警告 | `(´･_･`)` | `!` |
    
    ## 实现边界
    
    `src/logging.ts` 负责格式化、动作识别、字段拆分和颜色。业务层仍决定哪些事件属于 summary、standard 或 diagnostic。所有 `Error` 参数只输出 message，不在标准日志展开堆栈；底层 Provider 的重复警告已降为 debug，最终错误由业务时间线统一报告。
    
    `blindMode.enabled=true` 时，HDSI 不使用上述业务时间线：普通运行、错误详情、预览与模型侧调试都会静默，仅由服务层按配置间隔输出不含故事内容的健康心跳。失明模式不会改写其它 Koishi 插件的日志策略。
    
    ## 测试
    
    `test/logging.test.ts` 固定验证用户消息、模型开始、Alter 触发、错误、ANSI 颜色和简洁符号，防止后续改动重新退化成字段堆叠。

not ok 527 - release-facing files share the runtime version
  ---
  duration_ms: 14.091328
  type: 'test'
  location: '/tmp/hds-m4-baseline/upstream/hds-interlude/test/release-consistency.test.ts:2:1602'
  failureType: 'testCodeFailure'
  error: 'docs/development/logging/LAYERED_LOG_IMPLEMENTATION.md'
  code: 'ERR_ASSERTION'
  name: 'AssertionError'
  expected:
  actual: |-
    # 分层彩色日志实现
    
    适用版本：`1.0.1-rc50`
    
    ## 目标
    
    标准日志围绕用户能理解的任务时间线组织：收到事件、模型处理、完成、投递。故事 ID、队列、游标、周期扫描、计时器和 SQLite 临时重试只在 diagnostic 模式出现。
    
    ## 格式
    
    `logging.format` 支持：
    
    - `layered`：默认。阶段主行配合 `├─ / └─` 字段树。
    - `compact`：兼容脚本解析的单行文本。
    - `detailed`：兼容旧配置的分行文本。
    
    `colors=true` 使用 ANSI 语义色，`colorTheme` 提供两套高对比 256 色调色板：
    
    - `dark`：柔亮青蓝、薄荷绿、粉紫、暖金和珊瑚红，避免深色背景上的灰暗色。
    - `light`：深蓝、墨绿、深紫、赭金和暗红，避免白色背景上的浅色发虚。
    
    日志由服务端生成，无法读取 Koishi Console 的 CSS 主题，因此必须在 Console 手动选择。`kaomoji=false` 时改用简洁符号。
    
    ## 固定动作映射
    
    | 动作 | 颜文字 | 简洁符号 |
    | --- | --- | --- |
    | 接收 | `(*^▽^*)` | `←` |
    | 处理中 | `(•̀ᴗ•́)و` | `⋯` |
    | 完成 | `(ﾉ´ヮ`)ﾉ*: ･ﾟ` | `✓` |
    | 投递 | `(・ω・)ノ` | `→` |
    | Alter 触发 | `(๑•̀ㅂ•́)و✧` | `⚡` |
    | 情绪偏移 | `(*>ω<*)` | `★` |
    | 记忆 | `₍ᐢ- ˕ -ᐢ₎zzZ` | `◈` |
    | 推进 | `(⊙ω⊙)` | `⟳` |
    | 主体节奏 | `ᕙ( •̀ ᗜ •́ )ᕗ` | `◇` |
    | 群聊 | `(´▽｀)ノ` | `◎` |
    | 错误 | `(˶ˊᜊˋ˶)` | `✗` |
    | 重试 | `(ง •̀_•́)ง` | `↻` |
    | 警告 | `(´･_･`)` | `!` |
    
    ## 实现边界
    
    `src/logging.ts` 负责格式化、动作识别、字段拆分和颜色。业务层仍决定哪些事件属于 summary、standard 或 diagnostic。所有 `Error` 参数只输出 message，不在标准日志展开堆栈；底层 Provider 的重复警告已降为 debug，最终错误由业务时间线统一报告。
    
    `blindMode.enabled=true` 时，HDSI 不使用上述业务时间线：普通运行、错误详情、预览与模型侧调试都会静默，仅由服务层按配置间隔输出不含故事内容的健康心跳。失明模式不会改写其它 Koishi 插件的日志策略。
    
    ## 测试
    
    `test/logging.test.ts` 固定验证用户消息、模型开始、Alter 触发、错误、ANSI 颜色和简洁符号，防止后续改动重新退化成字段堆叠。
